## 2. Global Summary

- `topic`: ARM Cortex-M7 Processor Technical Reference Manual for processor revision r0p2.
- `module_scope`: Cortex-M7 core architecture, component blocks, programmers model, system control registers, initialization, memory system, MPU, NVIC, FPU, debug/CoreSight, CTI, DWT, ITM, and fault detection/handling.
- `key_chapters`: Chapter 1 Introduction; Chapter 2 Programmers Model; Chapter 3 System Control; Chapter 5 Memory System; Chapters 6-8 MPU/NVIC/FPU; Chapters 9-12 Debug/CTI/DWT/ITM; Chapter 13 Fault detection and handling; Appendix A Revisions.
- `key_terms`: Cortex-M7, ARMv7E-M, Thumb, DPU, PFU, LSU, FPU, NVIC, WIC, MPU, ETM, ITM, DWT, FPB, CTI, TCM, ITCM, DTCM, AXIM, AHBP, AHBS, AHBD, PPB, SCS, ECC, MBIST, CoreSight.
- `summary`: The document defines Cortex-M7 processor functionality, configurable implementation options, programmer-visible behavior, bus and memory interfaces, register maps, cache/TCM behavior, debug and trace units, and safety-related fault detection mechanisms. This Manifest indexes physical pages, outline paths, tables, figures, symbols, and short anchors for source-PDF retrieval.
